### Page 2

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
⚫ 美國 9 月通膨數據高於市場預期，CPI 月增 0.6%、年增 3.7%；核心 CPI 月增
0.3%、年增 2.6%。同月 PPI 月增 1.3%，創 2022 年 3 月以來最大增幅，年增
6.3%，顯示通膨壓力已逐步由能源價格擴散至更廣泛領域。數據公布後，市場一
度提高對聯準會 10 月升息的預期，但隨後多位官員釋出審慎訊號，使升息押注
降溫。聯準會理事鮑曼表示，決策前仍有時間觀察先前升息效果；舊金山聯準銀
行總裁戴莉則認為 10 月無立即升息之必要。而褐皮書顯示，美國就業市場維持
「低招聘、低裁員」格局，而高能源價格帶來的通膨壓力仍值得關注。
⚫ 中國 9 月通膨持續回升，CPI 年增 0.9%，創近三個月高點；PPI 年增 5.0%，創
逾四年最大漲幅，顯示通縮壓力進一步減輕。主要受能源與原物料價格上漲帶
動，且 9 月進出口表現優於預期，AI 產業鏈需求與貿易環境改善提供支撐；不

過，信貸增長仍仰賴政府發債與季底效應，內需與民間融資動能仍偏弱。
⚫ 日本銀行審議委員小枝淳子表示，不宜對升息過於保守，並認為政策利率仍有進
一步上調空間。總裁植田和男則強調維持 2%通膨目標的重要性。整體而言，日
本央行持續釋出偏鷹派訊號，有助支撐日圓表現。
⚫ 歐洲央行管委溫施表示，在能源價格波動下，為了控制通膨仍不排除進一步升息
可能，總裁拉加德則認為法國債務問題應透過財政政策解決，歐洲央行官員普遍
認為能源價格的潛在波動依然是推升通膨並使其居高不下的主要風險來源。。
⚫ 澳洲央行會議紀錄指出，能源價格上漲與需求韌性可能使通膨更具黏性，保留
進一步升息空間。然而 9 月就業數據意外疲弱，市場大幅下調升息預期，目前
預估 11 月維持利率不變的機率接近 90%。

### Page 3

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
美元兌新台幣匯率走勢
Oct/19/2026
＜上週走勢＞
上週台幣匯價高點為 31.890，低點在 32.520，以 32.338 作收，較前一周收盤價
貶值 4 角 6.3 分。
上週台幣走貶，周初，外資延續前周大幅匯出的走勢，雖逢高吸引出口商拋匯，
仍不敵外資匯出力道令台幣走貶；週二台股重挫近千點，外資大幅賣超台股 1653 億
並匯出，台幣接連跌破 32.000、32.100 及 32.200，總成交量 71.3 億，創歷史新高；
周三台股收漲，外資雙向進出，台幣收斂部分跌幅；週四，受美國通膨高於預期影
響，早盤台幣跳空開低，台股跌逾 2%，外資大幅賣超 3025 億，台幣跌破 31.400 關
卡，總成交量 91.2 億，再創歷史新高；週五外資持續匯出，台幣貶至周內低點 32.520，
終場在央行調節之下收在 32.338。
＜未來分析＞
預估本週台幣走勢偏弱。就國際市場而言，美國 CPI、PPI 雙雙高於預期，通

膨壓力仍在，儘管聯準會官員陸續發言表示不急於升息，淡化市場對於聯準會十月
升息預期，惟年底前聯準會仍有升息一碼空間，利率持穩於高位將給予國際美元支
撐；就台灣市場來看，市場對 AI 產業資本支出、融資成本及信用風險產生疑慮，若
賣壓延續，將壓抑市場風險偏好，增加外資調整台股部位及匯出的壓力，令台幣面
臨持續貶值的壓力，惟出口商拋匯將抑制台幣貶值速度，綜合以上，預估本週台幣
走勢偏弱。
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：32.100-32.700

### Page 4

4  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌人民幣匯率走勢  
Oct/19/2026 
＜上週走勢＞ 
上週人民幣震盪走升。上週人民幣中間價連續走升，週三中間價升至 6.7249 創
三年八個月新高，惟市場靜待美國公布通膨數據，交投謹慎，人民幣僅小幅走升；
週三晚間，美國公布 9 月 CPI 年增率 3.7%、核心 CPI 年增 2.6%，雙雙高於預期，
帶動美元指數升至年內高點 102.945、人民幣貶至周內低點 6.7005；然多位聯準會官
員發言表示聯準會不急於升息，令市場對於聯準會十月升息預期略有降溫，美元指
數自高點回落，激勵人民幣由貶轉升；臨近周末，聯準會主席華許於 IMF 會議發言
並未指引十月聯準會政策利率走向，再度淡化市場對於聯準會十月升息預期，國際
美元走勢偏弱，人民幣走升觸及年內高點 6.6825，終場境外人民幣收在 6.6850，週
線升值 91 點，境內人民幣則收在 6.6870，週線升值 60 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣震盪偏升。就國際市場來看，美國仍面臨高通膨、年底可能將
再次升息，利差因素支撐美元指數於高檔震盪，限縮人民幣升值空間；就中國國內
市場來看，中間價持續偏升將帶動人民幣走升，加以出口商實質拋匯需求將支撐人
民幣表現；最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH 日線圖中，匯價維持在五日均線之下交
投，均線呈現空頭排列，加以 USD/CNH 有效跌破 6.6900 整數關卡，顯示 USD/CNH
空方勢力較盛，綜合以上，預估本週人民幣震盪偏升。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6500-6.7000

### Page 5

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Oct/19/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週 USD/JPY 先上後下。週初，受油價攀升推升美國通膨與利率預期帶動，美
元指數溫和走升創 6 月底以來新高，推升 USD/JPY 突破 158.50；週間，美國公布 9
月 CPI 高於預期，帶動美元指數升至 2025 年 5 月以來高點，也使 USD/JPY 最高來
到 158.94，然逢高獲利了結賣壓出籠使匯價回落；臨近週末，USD/JPY 一度下探至
157.53，隨後逢低買盤進場收復部分跌幅，終場收在 157.99，週線下跌 13 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、受美伊戰事推升汽柴油飆漲
與服務成本擴散影響，美國 9 月 CPI 及 PPI 月增漲幅均超出市場預期，加上初請失
業金人數連五週低於 20 萬人顯示勞動市場穩定，市場預期聯準會將於 12 月再次升
息，美日實質利差維持高檔，持續為 USD/JPY 提供下檔支撐。二、被視為鴿派的日
本經濟財政大臣城內實明確表態，貨幣政策應交由日本央行自行判斷，並宣告日本
已擺脫長期通縮、轉型為增長導向經濟體。此番言論降低政治干預日銀進一步升息
的疑慮，在貨幣政策正常化的腳步下，預計將限縮 USD/JPY 的上行空間。三、就技
術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY 匯價目前交投於月線及年線之間，各天期均線高度
糾結，多空雙方於 158 關卡附近拉鋸，在有效突破季線及年線反壓或跌破月線支撐
前，仍以區間震盪看待。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY 本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 美元兌日圓：157.00-159.00 
前週交易區間 
157.53 158.94

### Page 6

6  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
歐元兌美元匯率走勢  
         Oct/19/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週 EUR/USD 震盪走升。週初，持續受法國財政赤字與歐債利差擔憂拖累，
EUR/USD 震盪走弱；週間，因美國 9 月 CPI 高於預期，市場重新評估聯準會年底前
升息腳步，導致 EUR/USD 跳水最低來到 1.1112；臨近週末，儘管美國 PPI 與零售
銷售數據偏熱，市場優先反映美債殖利率回落與風險資產重新定價，美元多頭獲利
了結帶動 EUR/USD 谷底反彈，最高來到 1.1269，終場收在 1.1239，週線上漲 42 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD 區間偏空震盪，主要原因如下：一、法國央行月度展望報
告指出，法國第三季 GDP 預估僅微幅成長 0.1%，印證近期高能源的成本已實質拖
累歐元區核心成員國增長。同時，歐洲央行總裁拉加德明確拒絕出手干預法債，要
求法國透過自身財政政策化解赤字困境；在明年法國大選前的財政隱憂與基本面疲
弱下，預計將令歐元上檔持續承壓。二、儘管歐洲央行管委溫施與總裁拉加德保留
12 月升息預期，加上德國基建與軍費支出為歐元提供部分下檔保護，但市場穩固預
期聯準會年底前將再升息一次。在美國經濟韌性與高利差優勢主導下，預計將限縮
歐元反彈空間。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD 自低點反彈並站回五日線
之上，然而中長期均線下彎仍維持空頭排列，RSI 指標亦處於 50 中軸下方，顯示整
體空方趨勢尚未扭轉，反彈至月線附近恐面臨較大賣壓。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD
本週呈現區間偏空震盪的可能性較高。 
＜預估本週＞ 歐元兌美元：1.1150-1.1300 
前週交易區間 
1.1112 1.1269

7  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
                           新台幣利率走勢分析

115. 10.12-115.10.16 
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.805% ~ 0.830%  
30 天期短票利率   1.67% ~ 1.68% 
公債－10 年期-A15109     2.020~2.025% 
－ 5 年期- A15108 1.88%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分，本週央行連續減發定存單使市場資金寬鬆，雖外資大舉匯
出，資金供給仍充裕，帶動短率朝區間低位移動。 
週五央行標售 2 年期定存單 250 億元，加權平均得標利率為 1.58%，低於市場
預期區間 1.60%-1.62%，較上期標售結果下跌 5.8 個基本點。 
週 五 隔 夜 拆 款 利 率 區 間 在 0.805%~0.830% ， 30 天 期 短 票 主 要 成 交 在
1.67%~1.68%，利率大致持平於上週。 
二、 5 年期指標券 A15108 週二搓合成交在 1.88%，後隨美債利率滑落，加上本週
2 年期公債標債結果提振市場持券信心，帶動 10 年期指標券 A15109 週四向
下成交在 2.02%~2.025%。 
在銀行去化短期資金及補充 LCR 之需求下，週三 2 年期公債得標利率為 1.65%，
落在市場預估區間下緣，投標倍數回升至 2.86 倍，銀行業得標比重上升至 88%。
股市承壓帶動避險需求，美債利率大幅回檔，促使台債交投偏空氛圍轉趨和
緩，惟在央行維持緊縮貨幣政策下，買盤積極追價的意願不強，預期短線台債
殖利率呈區間格局的可能性較高，關注下週 10 年券標售結果，預估 5 年券區
間為 1.85%~1.90%，10 年券區間為 1.98%~2.03%。

中華民國一一五年十月二十七日

國際經濟金融週報

### Page 2

本報告係根據研究資料及各項可靠數據作成，兆豐銀行保留報告內容所有權利，未經本行許可，請勿引
用相關內容。

1

國際經濟金融週報內容摘要 
美 
國 
企業活動維持擴張，消費信心與房市疲弱，通膨及關稅風險仍存 
經濟 
⚫ 10 月美國綜合 PMI 產出指數初值由 58.4 降至 57.6，企業活動仍強勁擴
張，內需及出口帶動訂單與就業成長；惟油價、關稅及供應鏈瓶頸推升
成本，售價漲幅創 2022 年 8 月以來新高，通膨壓力持續升溫，後續須
關注聯準會政策動向。 
⚫ 9 月美國住宅市場續顯疲弱，建築許可及新屋開工分別年減 3.0%及
7.4%，反映住宅投資動能放緩。儘管多戶住宅許可與完工數回升，但整
體營建活動仍偏弱，後續仍須關注房貸利率、住房負擔能力及營建成本
對市場復甦的影響。 
⚫ 密西根大學公布之 10 月美國消費者信心指數降至 48.0，月減 0.2%、年
減 10.4%，顯示民眾對目前經濟環境仍缺乏信心，雖然對未來景氣的看
法略有改善，但高物價與借貸成本仍讓消費者感到壓力。此外，未來一
年及長期通膨預期分別升至 4.7%及 3.5%，反映民眾仍擔心物價持續上
漲。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 10 月 23 日川普宣布調整加拿大商品 50%關稅清單，10 月 30 日生效，
豁免部分紙製品、機械及包裝材料，並新增地毯、油漆等課稅項目。外
媒認為此舉與期中選舉有關，有助降低部分企業成本，但美加貿易摩擦
仍未化解。 
短評 
⚫ 10 月美國綜合產出指數雖略為下滑，但仍維持高檔，顯示內需與就業持
續支撐企業活動擴張。然而，消費者信心仍處低檔，反映高物價與借貸
成本持續壓抑購買意願，儘管民眾對未來景氣的看法略有改善，但新屋
開工及建築許可同步下滑，顯示消費與住宅投資前景仍面臨挑戰。另一
方面，美國調整部分加拿大商品關稅，雖有助減輕部分企業進口成本，
但雙邊貿易摩擦尚未化解，政策不確定性仍存。

兆豐國際商業銀行 徵信處

一、 美國—企業活動維持擴張，消費信心與房市疲弱，通膨及關稅風險仍存 
標普全球(S&P Global)公布，2026 年 10 月美國綜合 PMI 產出指數(Composite 
PMI Output Index)初值由上月的 58.4 降至 57.6，其中服務業採購經理人指數
(Services PMI)由 58.8 降至 57.9，製造業採購經理人指數(Manufacturing PMI)則由
55.9 微升至 56.0，創 2022 年 5 月以來新高，企業活動雖略有放緩，但仍維持強
勁擴張。需求方面，內需穩健及出口改善帶動整體新訂單增速創逾 4 年半新高，
商品出口訂單亦為 2025 年 6 月以來首次增加；企業持續擴增人力，整體就業增
速為 2022 年 6 月以來次高。另一方面，未完成訂單增加、供應商交貨延遲加劇，
加上油價上漲及關稅推升能源、運輸與原物料成本，使企業投入成本維持高檔，
帶動售價漲幅升至 2022 年 8 月以來最高。儘管企業對未來一年營運前景的樂觀
程度略降，但仍處於今年相對高檔。整體而言，美國經濟在內外需及就業支撐下
維持擴張，惟供應鏈瓶頸與成本上升加劇通膨壓力，後續仍須關注物價走勢及聯
準會貨幣政策動向。 
密西根大學公布，2026 年 10 月美國消費者信心指數終值降至 48.0，略低於
上月的 48.1，月減 0.2%、年減 10.4%，並較今年 2 月下降約 15%，顯示消費者信
心持續處於低檔。分項來看，當前經濟狀況指數由 50.9 降至 46.7，月減 8.3%、
年減 20.3%；消費者預期指數則由 46.3 回升至 48.8，月增 5.4%、年減 3.0%，反
映民眾對未來景氣的看法略有改善，但整體信心仍偏弱。調查指出，高利率及購
買負擔壓力使耐久財購買條件下降 18%，拖累當前經濟狀況指數；未來一年企業
景氣預期雖改善約 14%，但仍低於去年同期 16%。通膨預期方面，未來一年通膨
預期由 4.6%升至 4.7%，長期通膨預期亦由 3.4%升至 3.5%，雙雙連續第二個月攀
升，創今年 5 月以來最高水準。密西根大學指出，2025 年關稅政策衝擊及 2026
年伊朗衝突引發的能源價格上漲，使民眾通膨預期再度升溫，且長期預期已明顯
高於 2019 年及 2024 年水準。整體而言，10 月美國消費者信心大致持平，但高物
價與借貸成本仍壓抑購買意願，加上短期及長期通膨預期持續上升，可能增加物
價回穩的難度，後續仍須關注能源價格、家庭消費及通膨預期變化對聯準會貨幣
政策的影響。

2026 年 9 月美國住宅市場建築許可年化總數降至 140.0 萬戶，月減 0.2%、年
減 3.0%，其中單戶住宅建築許可降至 84.5 萬戶，月減 4.0%、年減 4.8%，惟 5 戶
以上多戶住宅建築許可增至 50.3 萬戶，月增 5.9%、年增 1.0%，顯示不同住宅類
型開發規劃表現分歧。新屋開工年化數降至 122.1 萬戶，月減 5.2%、年減 7.4%，
其中單戶住宅開工 85.9 萬戶，較去年同期仍增 2.6%，但多戶住宅開工降至 34.9
萬戶，年減 25.9%，拖累整體營建動能。施工方面，在建住宅維持 128.0 萬戶，與
上月持平但較去年減少 3.3%；完工年化數則回升至 119.2 萬戶，月增 2.2%，主要
受多戶住宅完工月增 14.9%帶動，惟整體仍較去年同期減少 14.0%。整體而言，
美國住宅市場呈現建築許可及新屋開工下滑、在建規模持平及完工略有回升的態
勢，反映住宅營建活動仍偏弱，後續仍須關注房貸利率走勢、住房負擔能力及營
建成本變化對住宅市場復甦的影響。 
美國總統川普 10 月 23 日依據《1930 年關稅法》第 338 條簽署公告，調整部
分加拿大商品加徵 50%關稅的適用範圍，並自 10 月 30 日起生效。此次調整取消
膠合板、部分紙製品、機械與冷凍設備、包裝用木箱及紙箱、新鮮與乾燥海藻等
商品的額外關稅，同時新增閥門、地毯、油漆、洗髮精、可可粉、木製棺材、空
調設備及獨木舟等課稅項目，呈現局部鬆綁與新增課稅並行的政策方向。白宮表
示，此舉旨在減輕加拿大貿易措施對美國企業造成的負擔，並維護國內產業利益。
由於緬因州農業及製造業高度依賴加拿大進口物資，共和黨參議員柯林斯(Susan 
Collins)積極向白宮爭取關稅豁免，外媒亦將此次調整與 11 月 3 日期中選舉連結。
整體而言，新措施有助降低部分美國企業的進口成本，但美加貿易摩擦尚未完全
化解，後續仍須關注雙邊談判及關稅政策對供應鏈的影響。 
二、歐元區—法國動盪短期內難降溫，料將使央行保持審慎觀望態度 
 歐元區 10 月綜合採購經理人指數(PMI)下降 1.3 點至 51.9，抵銷上月的強勁
增幅，但為連續五個月高於 50 的榮枯線，主要係受到法國大規模抗議活動的影
響，使法國綜合 PMI 下降 2.7 點至 48.4；此外，德國綜合 PMI 下降 1 點至 52.8，
亦反映近期通膨壓力增加，使民眾傾向保守消費。若以產業區分，歐元區服務業、
製造業 PMI 分別降 1.3、0.5 點至 51.7、52.9；惟德國製造業 PMI 上升至相當強勁
的 54.2，反映德國政府擴張軍備投資以及基礎建設之需求，持續推動製造業生產
動能。

呈現上升趨勢，並對景氣產生暫時性的抑制效果。在此背景下，市場預期歐洲央
行在 10 月 28 日的政策會議將採取觀望立場，後續關注歐洲央行是否將透過政策
訊號或流動性工具，為平息法國主權債務利率而採取行動。 
三、日本—貿易逆差及通膨壓力延續，內需仍具韌性 
 9 月出口雖維持強勁，受半導體製造設備及汽車出口增加所帶動，出口額年增
16.7%；惟進口增幅更為顯著，9 月進口額年增 18.9%，其中原油及液化天然氣進
口額分別大增 51.1%及 39.2%，主要反映中東局勢推升能源價格，以及日本尋求
替代能源供應來源的影響。由於進口額高於出口額，9 月貿易逆差達 5,365 億日
圓，為連續第 5 個月呈現逆差；累計 2026 上半財年（4 至 9 月）貿易逆差亦較上
年同期擴大 98.8%至 2.82 兆日圓。整體而言，出口並未明顯轉弱，近期貿易逆差
擴大主要反映能源進口成本攀升，使進口增幅持續高於出口。 
 9 月全國消費者物價指數(CPI)年增 2.1%，即使政府透過能源及教育補貼等措
施抑制物價，通膨仍高於 2%，顯示實際通膨動能更為強勁。從物價結構來看，石
油、原物料及工資成本上升已逐步傳導至終端價格，漲價範圍由家具、醫療用品
及休閒用品等商品，進一步擴散至房屋維護、公共運輸、住宿及外食等服務項目，
顯示企業成本轉嫁範圍逐步擴大，通膨壓力亦由商品向服務領域擴散。隨通膨持
續高於 2%且漲價範圍擴大，亦為日本央行進一步推動貨幣政策正常化提供支撐。 
 10 月綜合 PMI 持平於 52.3，顯示經濟仍維持穩定擴張，其中製造業 PMI 下
降 0.8 點至 53.3，產出指數亦降至 3 月以來最低，主要反映新訂單及出口訂單成
長放緩，加上稀土金屬等原料供應受限，對生產形成一定壓力，惟生產與訂單仍
處擴張區間，尚未顯示製造業景氣明顯轉弱；服務業 PMI 回升 0.7 點至 52.0，新
業務及出口訂單同步改善，顯示服務需求仍具韌性，為支撐景氣的重要動能。另
一方面，能源、薪資及原物料成本居高不下，使投入價格壓力持續偏高，並逐漸

四、中國—第三季經濟成長率回升，但前景仍較為不利 
2026 年第三季 GDP 年增率初估值為 4.5%，較第二季的 4.3%上揚 0.2 個百分
點；累計 1-9 月 GDP 年增率則為 4.6%。第三季的二、三級產業年增率分別較第
二季上揚 0.4、0.1 個百分點至 3.4%，其中製造業、批發零售業年增率分別達 5.9%、
2.3%為主要動能，抵銷建築業年減 4.4%等拖累因素影響。整體而言，第三季 GDP
年增率上揚有部分受到中秋國慶連假落在 9 月末段(去年同期則於 10 月 1 日開
始)，致基期效果發揮部分影響，相關消費動能仍可能會在 10 月出現暫時性的回
落。 
在需求指標方面，(1)1-9 月累計零售額年增率為 1.1%，持平於 1-8 月的累計
年增率。其中 9 月零售額年增率增加 0.7 個百分點至 1.1%，部分受到新手機上市
帶動買氣，9 月通訊器材消費年增率高達 31.1%，加上中秋國慶長假帶動食品消
費年增率增加 1 個百分點至 2.1%所致；(2)1-9 月固定資產投資年減幅維持於 7.2%，
反映供應鏈移轉、政府主導去化過剩產能等因素導致民間投資需求急遽萎縮；萎
縮產業包含化工、醫藥、專用設備、汽車製造業、金屬製品業等；(3)1-9 月房地
產開發投資額年減幅由 19.9%擴大至 20.6%、施工面積年減幅為 12.8%，新建商
品房銷售面積年減幅擴大為 13.2%，房市投資、買氣皆持續低迷。 
受到出口維持高度成長帶動，9 月工業生產年增率較 8 月增加 0.3 個百分點
至 5.5%(累計 1-9 月工業生產年增率持平 1-8 月水準為 5.3%)。依產業別來看，9
月主要受到煤礦開採、化工、化纖等產業擴張影響，主因中秋與國慶長假前發電
業充實庫存水位，促使煤炭企業加快復產，加上油價上漲帶動相關中下游供應鏈
增產；惟高科技產品包含 IC、機器人、新能源車等年增率皆有放慢，反映去年中
秋國慶連假皆落在 10 月，使得去年 9 月工作日較多，致基期相對墊高的影響。 
展望未來，儘管政府 10 月初宣布釋放 5,500 億元人民幣的地方政府債務結存
限額，但額度下達到資金撥付往往需要數月時間，預期對於第四季的經濟影響有
限，使今年全年經濟成長率預估將落在 4.5%附近。此外，受歐盟右派勢力崛起及
保護主義影響，歐盟對中國貿易政策可能持續趨嚴，後續仍須關注雙方談判及溝
通結果，未來若外需動能減弱，預期中國政府將持續透過發展特定高科技產業及
扶植內需來支持經濟成長動能，以減輕房市不振及貿易環境惡化的衝擊。

8

五、印度及東南亞—新、馬出口保持強勁增速，菲律賓央行大舉升息 
(1)印度: 10 月 HSBC 綜合採購經理人指數（PMI）初值上升 0.8 點至 56.7，其中，
製造業 PMI 略下滑 0.2 點至 54.9、服務業 PMI 則上揚 1.0 點至 56.2，皆保持穩健
擴張態勢。由於美國、英國、中東及孟加拉等地的訂單增加，帶動新訂單及出口
訂單加速成長，製造商採購庫存增速創八個月來最快、成品庫存增速亦創歷史新
高，然而聘僱指數降至今年以來最低、未完成訂單下降，反映現階段人力充足。
另一方面，投入成本漲幅連續第七個月下降並降至近一年低點，產品售價漲幅亦
明顯放緩，通膨壓力緩解，有助提升企業獲利能力及消費者購買力，並且給予印
度央行（RBI）貨幣政策空間。 
(2)新加坡：9 月非石油國內出口（NODX）年增 68.4%，增速史上最快，連續第
23 個月正成長，其中，人工智慧需求強勁，帶動電子產品出口躍增 107.6%；非電
子產品出口亦大幅成長 54.2%，主要由船舶和遊艇組件所帶動。按出口目的地劃
分，對香港出口激增 121.1%、對台灣、韓國出口增幅達 96%，對美出口成長 73.2%，
但對印度出口下降 5.6%。展望未來，新加坡金融管理局在 10 月的宏觀經濟評估
報告中表示，直至 2027 年人工智慧 (AI) 驅動的成長將保持相對穩定，支持未
來幾季經濟成長表現。由於基數較高以及人工智慧相關投資預計放緩，全球利率
上升可能會對新加坡主要貿易夥伴的消費和非人工智慧投資造成壓力，可能會影
響外部需求，薪資成長放緩、招聘謹慎以及運輸成本上升可能會在 2026 年下半
年對可自由支配支出和零售活動構成壓力，預計 2027 年 GDP 成長將放緩。 
(3)印尼：印尼央行（BI）10 月會議連續第四次會議維持基準利率(7 天期逆回購利
率,BI-Rate)於 5.75%不變，貸款便利利率（LFR）維持在 6.50%不變，但將隔夜存
款便利利率（DFR）調高 25 個基點至 5%，旨在提高印尼盾資產的回報率，藉此
吸引並留住外資，同時宣布分兩階段調降存款準備率，11 月 1 日起下調 1 個百分
點至 8%，12 月 1 日起進一步降至 7%，以提供銀行體系流動性。整體而言，印尼
央行爲捍衛印尼盾，在 5 至 6 月間共升息 100 個基點，同時調降外資避險成本，
並採取「資金停留愈久、優惠愈高」的機制吸引外資流入。惟近期國際指數編纂
公司 MSCI 擬將印尼股市由”新興市場”降級為”獨立市場”，引發資金外流疑慮，
央行選擇利率按兵不動，搭配降準提供銀行體系流動性，以兼顧印尼盾穩定與國
內經濟需求，未來 MSCI 檢討進展(最快 2027 年 4 月公布)及印尼盾走勢，將持續
牽動央行後續政策。

(4)菲律賓：菲律賓央行（BSP）10 月 22 日宣布大幅調升基準利率(逆回購利率)2
碼至 5.50%，為 2023 年以來最大幅度升息。央行指出， 9 月通膨率已升至 7.2%，
遠高於 2%-4%的目標區間；同時核心通膨持續攀升，顯示價格上漲已由食品與能
源擴散至整體經濟。BSP 亦擔憂中東情勢推升國際油價、運輸費率調漲，以及聖
嬰現象衝擊農業生產，進一步推高物價。此外，披索貶至歷史低點附近，增加進
口通膨風險，因此採取較激進的緊縮措施以穩定市場預期，貨幣政策重點已由支
持經濟成長轉向優先遏制通膨風險。 
(5)馬來西亞：9 月出口額年增 54.5%至 MYR2,151 億元，其中電子&電氣產品出
口大增 69.2%，持續反映 AI 帶動的強勁需求，礦產品出口亦大幅成長 56.3%，抵
銷農產品出口續跌 5.7%的影響，對台灣、美國出口大增 175.2%、89.9%、對新加
坡出口增幅亦達 50.4%。9 月進口額成長 52.7%至 MYR1,814 億元，受惠出口引
申需求，中間產品、資本貨物進口分別成長 69.6%、20.9%，消費品進口轉為正成
長 18.2%，自中國和新加坡進口分別成長 67.6%、90.7%。進出口相抵，9 月貿易
順差達 MYR337 億元、年增 64.9%。 
六、台灣—外銷訂單創新高，產業景氣冷熱不均 
9 月外銷訂單達 1,142.1 億美元，再創單月新高，年增 62.4%，主要受惠於人
工智慧、高效能運算與雲端服務等應用蓬勃發展，以及消費性電子新品陸續推出，
帶動相關供應鏈維持高檔；加上國際原物料價格上漲，推升整體接單表現。惟各
產業接單動能仍呈分化，科技產品需求強勁，傳統產業景氣則相對疲弱。經濟部
指出，雖有年底節慶補貨潮挹注，但傳統產業部分產能過剩，終端需求仍未見明
顯回升。

Merida (9914.TW)
3Q Briefing Takeaway – New Model Cycle to Drive 2027 Recovery; SBC 
Visibility Remains Limited 
CITI'S TAKE 
We expect Merida’s core business to recover in 2027E, supported by 
accelerating new model launches and largely normalized EU inventories. 
However, we expect the recovery in 2027E to be gradual with US demand 
remaining sluggish. Meanwhile, despite improving pre-tax profits, SBC’s 
profitability remains highly uncertain. We trim our TP to NT$67 factoring in 
a lowered NTM P/E of 11x and earnings cuts. Remain Neutral as we see 
limited near-term catalysts and prefer to await clearer evidence of 
sustainable SBC profitability and stronger core earnings momentum.
Outlook: More new model launches in 2027E to drive a sales comeback — Mgmt. 
attributed softer shipments this year to deliberate reductions in existing models 
ahead of more new product launches in 2027E. Both Merida’s own brand and its 
major OEM customer, SBC, have adopted this strategy, aiming to preserve pricing 
discipline and avoid excessive discounting. Mgmt. expects the inventory 
adjustment to conclude by year-end and most new models are scheduled to begin 
shipping from Jan-27, paving the way for a sales recovery in 2027E.  
EU leads the recovery; China stabilizes while the US remains sluggish — Merida’s 
own brand grew 14% y/y in 9M26 in EU, supported by solid demand for road and 
gravel bikes. The mgmt. believe inventory normalization is largely complete in EU. 
China’s own brand sales grew 1% y/y in 9M26, better than the original expectation 
of DD% decline, helped primarily by entry and mid end bikes. Mgmt. expects another 
single digit sales growth in China in 2027E, supported by resilient commuter 
demand. The recovery of US market, on the other hand, is lagging EU and China amid 
inflationary pressure and persisting macro uncertainties.   
Still low visibility on SBC profitability —The weak bottom-line of SBC was primarily 
due to a conservative effective tax rate assumptions rather than a deterioration of 
its fundamentals. SBC’s 9M26 pre-tax profits grew 42% y/y or +20% y/y excluding 
US tariff refunds, reflecting improving operating performance and cost discipline. 
While improving pre-tax profitability is encouraging, we remain cautious on SBC’s 
near-term earnings contribution until there is a greater clarity on its normalized 
effective tax rate and the sustainability of US profitability. (continued)

Earnings revisions and implications — We lower our earnings estimates by 20%
for 2026E and 6% for 2027E/2028E, mainly reflecting weaker-than-expected core
sales and profitability, alongside continued uncertainty surrounding SBC’s
profitability. We cut our TP to NT$67 based on 11x 4Q26-3Q27E P/E, down from
15.5x, factoring in weaker-than-expected core business and low visibility of SBC.
While we acknowledge the potential for a stronger sales recovery in 2027E as

Merida accelerates its new model launches, we believe the recovery will be gradual
rather than a meaningful acceleration in the underlying market demand.
Meanwhile, SBC’s uncertain net profitability and lingering US market weakness
remain the key overhang.  We would prefer to stay on the sidelines, awaiting clearer
evidence of sustainable SBC net profitability and stronger earnings momentum
from the core business. Reiterate Neutral.

Bull/Bear: Merida (9914.TW)

### Page 5

Merida (9914.TW)
17 November 2026 Citi Research
5
Merida
Company description
Merida is the second-largest bike manufacturer in Taiwan. Founded in 1972,
Merida was initially involved in OEM manufacturing before it started
marketing bikes under its own Merida brand in 1986. Merida has expanded its
footprint globally through its Merida brand and three OEM brands:
Specialized (in which Merida has a 35% stake), Centurion (51% stake), and
Miyata (45%). Merida's own brand contributes around 30-40% of total

revenues. Merida has established a proprietary network of stores throughout
China, with 16 wholly owned subsidiaries and close to 2,000 retailers that sell
only Merida-branded bikes.
Investment strategy
We rate Merida shares as Neutral. Although bike inventories have normalised
in the major markets after aggressive clearance over the past 2 years, slower-
than-expected bike demand recovery continues to weigh on Merida's topline
momentum. The EU market appears to be where we should see better growth

for 2026E, while China market also turns better than expected from 2Q26; yet
with low visibility of SBC profit trend and limited positive catalysts in sight,
we expect the share price to be range bound.
Valuation
Our target price for Merida shares of NT$93 is based on a 4Q26-3Q27E P/E of
11x, a 50% discount to its historical average of 22x during pre-Covid years
(2014-2019), in view of soft order momentum as a result of slow recovery in
bike demand, a volatile margin trend, and low visibility of SBC.

Risks
Downside risks that could mean the Merida shares trade below our target
price include: 1) e-bike adoption slower than expected; 2) weaker restocking
demand; 3) lukewarm sell-through at SBC; and 4) a weaker macro.
Upside risks that could mean the Merida shares trade above our target price
include: 1) e-bike adoption faster than expected; 2) stronger restocking
demand; 3) stronger sell-through at SBC; and 4) a strong macro.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

Feedback
Potential to win next gen TPU projects, while other ASIC
customers remain undisclosed.
1. Major AI ASIC project (TPU) - potential to win next generation projects.
• TPU v8 shipment track, US$2.5bn revenue in 4Q26. Reiterated US$16bn
revenue in 2027, high end of guidance. As there are more customers than
chip supply, if ABF substrate supply improves, revenue can improve.
• TPU v9 EMIB-T substrate supply is also key, company is comfortable on yield

and output trajectory.
• It sees its TPU v9 project as a major TPU SKUs for the customer. MediaTek
revenue per chip is higher than it was in v8. The compute die is handled by
the customer.
• MediaTek said it is possible for the customer to use designed TPU (eg. v8t
and v9) for AI LLM customer's rack system, besides Google's internal use.
• On third generation (TPU v10), MediaTek is working with customer's internal
team, and has high confidence to win the project.

• New generation TPU compute die handled by customer. MediaTek will focus
on D2D inference, I/O die, and chip packaging technology.
• We maintain our view that Alchip is strong candidate for TPU compute die
backend design. Based on Alchip’s NRE revenue timing, we think the
compute die could be for the TPU v10.
2. Other ASIC customers - awaiting more disclosure: MediaTek still cannot
disclose the non-China CSP customer name or project details. The engagement with

China CSP is ongoing, but needs to comply with US BIS rules.
3. ASIC business financial profile - OP margin improvement: Mediatek said a major
customer wanted to drive to full COT (customer own tool), but with the chip's
complexity growing, it hopes to sustain ASIC margin in future generations. The
company reiterated its 3Q earning call comments that ASIC op. margin is 10% above
the previous average. It reiterated its op margin can improve to 20%+ in 2027 on

higher ASIC revenue contribution. We estimate ASIC op. margin at 25-30%.
4. Smartphone business - still challenging: High memory costs continue to impact
China demand. Blended ASP for MediaTek's SoC has risen on price hikes and better
product mix.
Reiterate OW & Top Pick: MediaTek continues to win new TPU projects with higher
revenue per chip, and so revenue growth may extend to 2030-31. The chance to
supply to AI LLM for end-customer's rack system via a major customer could also

Morgan Stanley appreciates your support in
the 2027 Extel Asia Research Survey.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
MediaTek (2454.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions: cost of equity 9.2%, intermediate growth
rate 12.0%, terminal growth rate 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Edge AI proliferates smartphone replacement cycle.
n Smartphone demand rises in China and other EMs.
n New products attract high demand, resulting in market share gains.
n Google's TPU spurs demand.
Risks to Downside

n Smartphone demand deteriorates in China and other EMs.
n Competition heats up, resulting in pricing competition.
n New products attract low demand, causing market share loss.
n Margin dilution is more severe.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Greater China Technology Semiconductors

Industry View Attractive
What’s Changed
Egis Technology Inc
(6462.TWO) From To

Himax Technologies Inc

(HIMX.O) From To
Price Target US$17.40 US$9.20
Shenzhen Goodix Technology
Co Ltd (603160.SS) From To
Price Target Rmb58.00 Rmb40.00
Higher memory costs are squeezing handset makers’ budgets for
displays, touch controllers and sensors. We expect weak
Android volumes to limit DDIC suppliers’ ability to pass on
higher wafer and packaging costs, even where new OLED
programs lift content per phone.
Android bears most of the decline. Our US hardware team (2026 AlphaWise

Smartphone Survey: Another Down Year That's Not Yet Priced In) expects 2027
smartphone shipments to fall 5%, versus 4% growth previously ( Exhibit 2 ). The
team's bear case is a global decline of 11%. The base case has Android down about
9% to 768mn units and iPhone up 7% to 270mn. Across the US, China, the UK and
Germany, 41% of owners were extremely likely to upgrade within 12 months, down
2ppt ( Exhibit 3 ). The survey implies a replacement cycle of 4.4 years in 2027, up

from 4.2. We expect Android supply chains to face the greatest pressure in 2027:
falling shipments, continued de-specing, and margin compression leave Android
OEMs and suppliers more exposed as brands attempt to offset higher component
costs through a combination of price increases and bill of materials (BOM)
reductions.
China's replacement demand is weakening. We expect a 7% decline in China
smartphone shipments in 2027, following an estimated 12% decline in 2026. Among

China survey respondents, 48% were extremely likely to upgrade within 12 months,
down from 49% last year. When asked why they chose their current phone, 17%
cited built-in AI, down from 22%, while 35% cited overall device quality, up from
32% ( Exhibit 4 ).
Brand switching favors Apple in China ( Exhibit 6 ). Among respondents in China,
Apple had the strongest net switching intention at +28% of its installed base. The
corresponding rates for Xiaomi, Oppo, Vivo and Honor ranged from -21% to -31%. A

shift in purchases toward Apple would put further further pressure on component
demand at these four Android brands.
Stock implications: Egis: Rating downgraded to UW and price target lowered from
NT$105 to NT$66; we estimate wider losses in 2026, lower our 2027 EPS estimate
25% and introduce 2028 estimates. Himax: Remain EW, price target down from US
$17.4 to US$9.2, 2026/27/28 EPS estimates down 17%/17%/33%. Goodix: Remain
UW, PT down from Rmb58 to Rmb40, 2026/27/28 EPS estimates down

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

Himax: Earnings Revision and Quarterly Financials
We lower 2026/27/28 EPS estimates 17%/17%/33%: We incorporate 2Q-3Q26 actual
results. We see continued weakness in the consumer market (especially smartphones) due
to elevated component costs and weak demand. Increasing material and foundry prices
might also affect Himax's margins, as we believe it is unlikely to pass through to clients.
However, we expect 2027 to see more contributions from OLED DDIC, OLED T-con

shipments and WiseEye opportunities, which should help revenue and gross margin.
Continued OLED shipments for a mainstream model and inventory builds for new
premium phones lifted combined LCD and OLED handset IC sales in 3Q26. Management
expects smartphone driver sales to decline Q/Q in 4Q26. Its LCD touch-and-display drivers
serve the entry and mid-range segments, in which pressure to cut component costs is
greatest.

Himax: Valuation Methodology
We remain EW and lower our price target to US$9.2: This factors in our lower
expectations for 2026-28 earnings. Our beta assumption is raised from 1.3 to 1.5 (leading
to an increased cost of equity from 9.9% to 11.0%); the increase in beta reflects the higher
uncertainties around end market demand and component / foundry / OSAT cost pressure.
Our terminal growth rate assumption is cut from 3.4% to 2.0%; the new terminal growth

rate is now more in line with the macro economic growth rate of global developed
economies over the longer term. Other residual income assumptions are unchanged,
including risk-free rate of 2%, risk premium of 6%, intermediate growth rate of 8.0%.  We
lower our bull/bear-case values from US$26.0/8.7 to US$13.7/4.6.

premium 6.0%), a medium-term growth rate of 8.0% and a terminal growth rate of 2.0%, in

lukewarm consumer tech demand have

strengthened its presence in the autos
market.

to weakness in the overall non-AI space,

strong price hikes. Increasing material and

clients.

Goodix: Estimate Revisions
We  lower our 2026/27/28 earnings estimates 14%/18%/5%. We factor in 2-3Q26 actual
results, and reduce our earnings forecasts to reflect weak smartphone end market
demand and cost pressures.  Smartphones accounted for 62.6% of 1H26 sales, and weaker
demand and chip pricing weighed on 3Q revenue. Xiaomi’s 18 Pro series uses two Goodix
touch controllers and its ultrasonic fingerprint sensor. Wider adoption of integrated OLED

touch and display drivers (TDDI) would displace standalone touch controllers. Goodix’s
own OLED TDDI was still in internal testing in October.

Goodix: Valuation Methodology
We maintain our UW rating. We lower our price target to Rmb40 from Rmb58: This is
based on a residual income model using our base-case forecasts. The lower price target
reflects our lower earnings estimate. We also lower our terminal growth rate assumption
from 4.9% to 2.5%. The new terminal growth rate is now more in line with the macro
economic growth rate of global economies over the longer term.

Other key RI assumptions are unchanged, including a cost of equity of 8.4%, an
intermediate growth rate of 16.5%. We lower our bull-case value to Rmb55 from Rmb80
and our bear-case value to Rmb21 from Rmb31.

Egis: Valuation methodology
We downgrade our rating to UW and lower our price target from NT$105 to NT$66:
This is our base-case scenario value derived from a residual income model. This reflects
the changes in our 2026-27 earnings estimates. We roll forward our valuation date from
2025 to 2026.
Changes in our RI assumptions include:
• Cost of equity is raised to 11.0% from 9.5% (beta raised from 1.25 to 1.50). The
increase in beta reflects the higher uncertainties around end market demand and

component / foundry / OSAT cost pressure
• Intermediate growth rate cut from 14.8% to 8.0%. The decrease in intermediate
growth rate reflects our more moderate view on the end market demand recovery
and new products ramp up schedule
• Terminal growth rate cut from 5.0% to 2.0%. The new terminal growth rate is now
more in line with the macro economic growth rate of global developed economies
over the longer term
Other key RI assumptions remain unchanged:

• Risk-free rate 2% and risk premium 6%
• Payout ratio of 40%
We lower our bull-case value from  NT$143 to NT$95 and bear-case value from NT$49 to
NT$30, implying 24x and 7x 2027 EPS, respectively, on our estimates.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

1 
3167 大量(Ta Liang)

報告日期：2026/11/12 研 究 員：翁浩軒 (Davy Wong)  
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要： 
 公司 TM4 背鑽機具技術領先優勢 
 成型機受惠光模塊建置需求放量成長 
 半導體及光通訊檢量測為新成長動能

一. 3Q26 毛利率優於預期，EPS 10.02 元 
大量 3Q26 營收 36.71 億(QoQ+28.4%、YoY+159.5%)，受惠於 PCB
對高階鑽孔機需求持續放量，營收創歷史新高，毛利率升至 50.17%
優於預期，主要因高階 PCB 機台及 TM3 出貨提升，稅後淨利 9.05
億(QoQ+66.62%、YoY+320.97%)，EPS 10.02 元。

二、AI 帶動高階 PCB 設備放量 
受惠於 AI 趨勢，鑽孔機及成型機需求均放量，大量之鑽孔機接單
提升，高階機型 2025 年出貨 501 台，至 2026 年 10 月底已出貨 1189
台，全年出貨近 1500 台，2027 年已確定訂單已約 600 台，仍有許
多仍在洽談中，而內層銅量測 2025 年為 TM2/TM3 系列，出貨 19
台，2026 年 TM2/TM3 出貨 51 台，TM4 已出貨 35 台，至年底仍有
10 台訂單待出，2027 年預估將全年轉為 TM4，因終端客戶向 PCB
廠指定用大量的 TM4，我們預估有望出貨近 100 台。成型機部分，
今年前三季營收比重 30%，今年出貨比預期高，主要因光模塊需
求提升，採 mSAP 製程需用 CCD 成型機，公司成型機品質及市占
率高，預估 2027 年成長性不小於鑽孔機。整體而言，公司 PCB 在
手訂單已上千台，2027 年鑽孔機及成型機預期仍有雙位數成長。

三. 2026 / 2027 年獲利創高，預估 EPS 31.34 元/ 65.01 元 
展望 2027 年，PCB 及半導體業務均大幅成長，AI 帶動高階 Server
板用量，PCB 鑽孔機、成型機需求強勁，高階 PCB 設備出貨維持
雙位數成長; 半導體業務方面，先進製程、先進封裝需求爆發，大
量跨入半導體領域營運逐步發酵，除了主流封裝外，CoWoS、
SoIC、FOPLP、CPO 產能擴增將帶動相關檢量測設備需求，預估大
量 2027 年半導體營收有望倍增。預估大量 2026 年營收 126.14 億
(YoY+148.41%)，毛利率 47.65%，稅後淨利 28.28 億(YoY+294.83%)，
EPS 31.34 元。預估 2027 年營收 221.39 億(YoY+75.71%)，毛利率
50.39%，稅後淨利 58.71 億(YoY+107.63%)，EPS 65.01 元。

四.投資建議：買進，目標價 1300 元 
大量為 PCB 鑽孔機、成型機大廠，為市場高階設備品質及技術領
導廠商，市占率亦高。過去設備出貨量隨景氣循環而波動明顯，
近年受惠於 AI 對高階 PCB 設備需求放量，加上切入半導體領域量
逐步發酵，公司檢量測設備於先進封裝之滲透率提升，加上 CPO
設備亦準備出貨，公司營運正向。考量到 1)高階 PCB 設備接單暢
旺、2)2027 年獲利翻倍成長、3)公司跨入先進封裝及 CPO 領域評
價有望提升，參考 2027 年 EPS 給予 20 倍本益比，目標價 1300 元，
建議買進。

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介 
大量科技股份有限公司成立於 1980 年，總部位於桃園，公司以自
行研發製造控制器為競爭核心技術，發展鑽孔機、成型機等設備，
控制器可作為降低成型機振動及提升鑽孔精準度。公司業務含概
PCB 及半導體，PCB 設備產品包含 PCB 鑽孔定位、PCB 成型切割、
點膠、曲線滾塗等塗佈製程電漿之蝕刻、鍍膜等表面改質製程等；
半導體設備事業已於 2026 年 8 月 1 日分拆為大量先進智能，目前
由大量 100%持有，產品涵蓋 AOI、量測及自動化設備。公司之生
產工廠包括桃園八德廠、大陸南京廠、蘇州漣水廠，客戶含蓋兩
岸 PCB 廠及台灣 Foundry 及 OSAT 廠，包括欣興、健鼎、瀚宇博，
中國 PCB 廠深南、景旺、超聲等，台灣競爭對手包括台灣恩得、
東台、德國 SCHMOLL、中國大族激光等。

二.產業趨勢概況 
1. 大量以內層銅深度量測建立背鑽技術門檻 
AI 算力需求對於資料傳輸要求提高，PCB 訊號傳輸加快下背鑽需
求提升，加上 PCB 層數增加對於背鑽精度要求亦提升。PCB 鑽孔
製程是經由通孔->填孔(通孔鍍銅)->背鑽，PCB 鑽孔將特定層數導
通後需要將多餘的銅柱鑽除，以降低訊號干擾，隨著 PCB 有效頻
寬從 Blackwell 28GHz 提升至 Rubin 56GHz，背鑽精度對殘銅要求
從±3mil(殘銅共振頻率約 45GHz)提升至±2mil(殘銅共振頻率
60GHz)，在背鑽機精度要求提高趨勢下，大量自製 TM4 量測機
可進行孔內深度量測，精準辨識孔內銅層/絕緣層實際位置，先建
立 3D 孔洞深度地圖後再進行背鑽，因此 TM4 量測機搭配大量自
製控制器與軟體的背鑽機成為高階 AI Server 背鑽的主流選擇，因
為 TM4 只與自有背鑽機整合銷售，不外搭外部機台，公司因此建
立背鑽技術門檻。

2. AI 帶動高階 PCB 設備放量 
大量主要產品以 PCB 設備為主，營收比重約 9 成，三大產品線包
含 PCB 鑽孔機、PCB 成型機及塗膠設備。鑽孔機涵蓋一般型與高
階型，差異在於是否有 CCD，另提背鑽量測設備(Stub Mapper) TM
系列，能將孔位精準量測，規劃背鑽深度地圖，精度可達正負 2mil; 
成型機涵蓋一般型與 CCD 高階成型機; 塗膠設備主要應用於
AI/AR 眼鏡邊緣及玻璃基板邊緣塗膠保護。鑽孔機部分，今年前三
季營收比重 62%，受惠於 AI 趨勢，Server 板均需要背鑽，大量之
鑽孔機接單提升，高階機型 2025 年出貨 501 台，至 2026 年 10 月
底已出貨 1189 台，全年出貨近 1500 台，2027 年已確定訂單已約
600 台，仍有許多仍在洽談中，而內層銅量測 2025 年為 TM2/TM3
系列，出貨 19 台，2026 年 TM2/TM3 出貨 51 台，TM4 已出貨 35
台，至年底仍有 10 台訂單待出，2027 年預估將全年轉為 TM4，因
終端客戶向 PCB 廠指定用大量的 TM4，我們預估有望出貨近 100
台。TM2/TM3 是單軸架構，適用前期樣品打樣；打樣獲得終端認
可、要進入批量量產時，必須採用四軸的 TM4 才能符合產能與
精度效率。成型機部分，今年前三季營收比重 30%，今年出貨比
預期高，主要因光模塊需求提升，採 mSAP 製程，需用 CCD 成型
機，公司成型機品質及市占率都高，預估 2027 年成長性不小於鑽
孔機。整體而言，公司 PCB 在手訂單台數已上千台，2027 年鑽孔
機及成型機預期仍有雙位數成長。

圖二、大量高階機種出貨

3. 半導體檢量測設備為新成長動能 
大量近年切入半導體領域，以大量先進智能(TLSmart)品牌行銷，
公司四大核心能力包括機構設計、快速設備控制、高精度量測與 
AI 檢測視覺系統、系統整合能力，產品線含蓋半導體前中後段，
主要提供 Metrology（量測）、AOI 檢測、自動化設備，公司在先
進封裝技術節點角色，包括 CoWoS：提供 CoW 及 oS 端各關鍵
站點的量測與檢驗設備; 3DIC / SoIC：提供三大量測系統（CMP Pad 
Metrology、Wafer Trim Gap Metrology、Wafer Bump Shift Edge Profile 
Metrology）; 面板級封裝（FOPLP / CoPoS）：因應 Chip 尺寸擴
大中介層需求轉向 Panel，推出支援 310×310mm、600×600mm、510
×515mm 之量測檢測設備，多數已達量產或小批量階段; 共同光學
封裝（CPO / 矽光子）：與美系國際 Tier 1 大廠共同開發 CPO OE 
與 Package 前處理系統、光學檢查機及後製程一體整合機，2026
年建第一條試產線，2027 下半年預估量產。半導體整體 BB 
Ratio>2，預估今年第四季出貨開始放量增加。

4. 今明年產能持續擴充 
公司目前自有產能已可達到 400 台/月，明年規劃提升至 500 台/
月以上，目前設備組裝時間方面，一般機型組裝約在 1 週以內，
高階機型、TM4 則約需 1.5 倍時間。公司除了自有工廠產能外，
亦接洽多家外包，2027 年如果外包認證順利產能有望達 600 台/月。

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

1、3Q26 毛利率優於預期，EPS 10.02 元 
大量 3Q26 營收 36.71 億(QoQ+28.4%、YoY+159.5%)，營收創歷史
新高，毛利率升至 50.17%優於預期，主要因高階 PCB 機台及 TM3
出貨提升，稅後淨利 9.05 億(QoQ+66.62%、YoY+320.97%)，EPS 10.02
元。

2、2026 / 2027 年獲利創高，預估 EPS 31.34 元/ 65.01 元 
預估大量 2026 年營收 126.14 億(YoY+148.41%)，毛利率 47.65%，
稅後淨利 28.28 億(YoY+294.83%)，EPS 31.34 元。預估 2027 年營收
221.39 億 (YoY+75.71%) ， 毛 利 率 50.39% ， 稅 後 淨 利 58.71 億
(YoY+107.63%)，EPS 65.01 元。

### Page 1

2026년 10월 29일 
기업분석

SK하이닉스 (000660) 
높아지는 이익의 눈높이, 달라지는 메모리 사이클

Buy(유지) 
목표주가 3,150,000원, 현재 주가(10/28): 1,487,000원 
▶ Analyst 박준영 jyp94@hanwha.com 3772-7481 / RA 김나우 now.kim@hanwha.com 3772-7710

3Q26 Review: HBM4 출하 확대와 예상보다 강한 가격 상승 
분기 매출액 104.6조원(QoQ +32%, YoY +328%), 영업이익 83.3조원(QoQ +38%, YoY +632%)을 기록하며 시장 기대
치를 상회함. 영업이익률은 전분기 대비 약 3%p 상승한 80%로 사상 최대 분기 실적을 달성함. 특히 DRAM ASP가 전
분기 대비 20% 후반 상승하며 당사 기존 예상치인 +23%를 상회한 점에 주목할 필요가 있음. NAND ASP 역시 20% 
중반 상승하며 예상보다 강한 가격 흐름을 나타냄. DRAM ASP 상승에는 기존 DRAM의 가격 인상과 함께 3분기부터 
본격화된 HBM4 양산 및 출하 확대에 따른 제품 믹스 개선 효과가 반영됨. 서버 DDR5 판매량 역시 전분기 대비 40% 
증가하며 실적 성장을 뒷받침함. 특히 HBM4는 기존 HBM3E 대비 높은 기술적 난도를 반영한 가격으로 공급되고 있어 
향후 출하 비중 확대에 따른 추가적인 ASP 상승 효과도 기대됨. 
이번 실적에서 중요한 부분은 예상보다 높은 가격 상승폭이 2027년 실적 추정치의 상향 가능성을 높이고 있다는 점임. 3
분기 DRAM ASP가 기존 추정치를 상회한 가운데 4분기에도 HBM4 출하 확대와 기존 DRAM의 가격 상승이 이어질 
것으로 예상되기 때문임. 동사는 4분기 DRAM 출하량이 전분기 대비 20% 중반 증가할 것으로 전망했으며, HBM4 출
하량이 HBM3E를 넘어 주력 제품으로 자리 잡을 것으로 예상함. 특히 HBM4의 양산 수율이 당초 목표를 상회하고 있
으며 높은 고객 수요에 대응하기 위해 생산성 극대화에 집중하고 있다는 점도 긍정적임. HBM4 출하 확대에 따른 믹스 
개선과 기존 DRAM 가격 상승이 동시에 진행되는 가운데 HBM과 일반 DRAM의 수익성 격차 역시 유의미하게 축소
될 것으로 예상됨. 이는 특정 제품에 대한 수익성 의존도가 낮아지면서 DRAM 사업 전반의 이익 체력이 강화되고 있음
을 의미함. 메모리 산업의 높은 영업 레버리지를 고려하면 예상보다 높은 ASP와 출하량 증가는 2027년 이익 추정치의 
추가 상향으로 연결될 가능성이 높다고 판단함.

공급 부족의 장기화와 LTA 확대, 메모리 산업의 이익 구조 변화 
이번 컨퍼런스콜에서 확인된 또 다른 중요한 변화는 공급 부족의 장기화와 장기공급계약(LTA) 확대임. 동사는 첨단 공정
의 기술적 난도 상승과 신규 생산시설 구축에 소요되는 시간을 고려할 때 메모리 공급이 수요를 충족하지 못하는 환경이 
2030년까지 지속될 것으로 전망함. 특히 글로벌 주요 고객사 14곳과 장기공급계약을 체결했으며, 2027~2028년 전체 생
산능력의 50% 이상에 해당하는 물량을 확보함. HBM의 경우 2029년까지의 물량이 이미 판매 확정된 상황으로 중장기 
수요 가시성이 상당히 높아졌다고 판단함. 주목할 부분은 LTA가 단순한 공급물량 확보를 넘어 메모리 업체의 사업 구조
와 이익 안정성을 변화시키고 있다는 점임. 계약 가격은 고객 및 제품별로 차이가 있으나 합의된 범위 내에서 시장 상황
을 반영하는 구조로 운영되고 있으며 예치금 등 실질적인 계약 이행력을 높이는 장치도 포함됨. 이는 장기적인 판매물량
을 확보하면서도 업황 상승에 따른 가격 인상 효과를 일정 부분 누릴 수 있다는 점에서 긍정적임.

### Page 2

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

2 
특히 전체 생산능력의 50% 이상을 LTA로 확보하면서 나머지 물량은 시장 상황에 따라 탄력적으로 대응할 수 있다는 점
에 주목할 필요가 있음. 장기계약을 통해 안정적인 매출 기반을 확보하는 동시에 미계약 물량을 활용해 추가적인 가격 
상승에 대응할 수 있기 때문임. 물론 장기계약이 메모리 가격 하락을 완전히 방어하는 것은 아니지만, 과거와 비교해 물
량 가시성과 고객사와의 협상력이 동시에 개선되고 있다는 점은 분명함. 과거 메모리 산업이 높은 이익을 창출하는 국면
에서도 낮은 밸류에이션을 적용 받았던 이유 중 하나는 이익의 지속성에 대한 불확실성이었음. 그러나 장기계약 확대와 
공급 제약의 장기화가 동시에 진행된다면 시장이 평가하는 이익의 지속성 역시 높아질 수 있음. 따라서 향후 주가 상승
은 단순한 EPS 추정치 상향뿐 아니라 이익 변동성 축소에 따른 밸류에이션 재평가가 동시에 진행되는 형태로 나타날 가
능성이 높다고 판단함. 
AI 인프라 투자에 대한 우려 역시 중장기 메모리 수요를 훼손할 가능성은 제한적이라고 판단함. 동사는 주요 하이퍼스케
일러의 클라우드 매출이 전년 대비 30~80% 성장하고 있으며 기업용 AI 솔루션 계약 확대와 클라우드 수주잔고 증가를 
통해 AI 투자의 사업적 성과가 확인되고 있다고 설명함. 특히 초기 AI 투자가 소수 빅테크에 집중되었던 것과 달리 최근
에는 일반기업의 자체 인프라 구축, 소버린 AI, 온프레미스 등으로 투자 주체가 다변화되고 있다는 점도 긍정적임. 특정 
사업자의 투자 속도 조절이 전체 AI 인프라 수요에 미치는 영향은 과거 대비 완화될 수 있기 때문임. AI 투자에 대한 우
려가 지속되는 상황에서도 실제 메모리 수요와 고객사들의 장기계약 확대가 확인되고 있다는 점에서 메모리 업황의 구조
적 성장에 대한 긍정적인 시각을 유지함.

HBM4 경쟁력 강화와 CAPEX 확대, 이익 성장과 주주환원의 선순환 
HBM4의 본격적인 출하 확대와 신규 생산능력 확보 역시 2027년 실적 성장의 중요한 기반이 될 전망임. 동사는 M15X
의 생산능력을 연말까지 최대 수준으로 확대할 계획이며 HBM4의 양산 수율 역시 당초 목표를 상회하는 등 안정적인 
생산체계를 확보하고 있음. 특히 HBM 수요가 공급능력의 두 배를 상회하는 상황에서 고객사들의 높은 선호가 이어지고 
있다는 점은 경쟁력 유지에 긍정적임. 최근 일부 고객사들이 HBM 적층 단수를 낮추는 방안을 검토하고 있으나 이는 공
급 부족 상황에서 제한된 물량을 효율적으로 활용하기 위한 전략으로 판단됨. 적층 단수 조정이 전체 HBM 수요 감소로 
직결되는 것은 아니며 오히려 후공정 생산능력에 대한 요구가 확대될 가능성도 존재함. HBM4E와 커스텀 HBM 개발 
역시 고객사 일정에 맞춰 진행되고 있어 차세대 제품에서도 경쟁력을 유지할 것으로 기대됨. 
한편 2027년 CAPEX는 선단 공정 전환과 용인 클러스터를 비롯한 국내외 생산 인프라 확충으로 전년 대비 증가할 전망
임. 다만 CAPEX 확대를 과거와 같은 공급 과잉의 신호로 해석할 필요는 없다고 판단함. 선단 공정의 기술적 난도 상승
과 고부가 제품 비중 확대에 따라 투자금액 증가가 실제 생산량 증가로 정비례하지 않기 때문임. 특히 동사는 신규 팹 
건설 일정을 앞당기면서도 클린룸은 순차적으로 개방하고 실제 생산능력 확대는 최종 수요와 수익성을 고려해 단계적으
로 진행할 계획임. 고객사의 투자 계획뿐 아니라 재무건전성과 자금조달 여력, 공급망 준비 상황까지 점검해 CAPEX 집
행에 반영한다는 점도 긍정적임. 이는 AI 인프라 투자 확대에 대응하면서도 과거 메모리 산업의 공급 과잉 위험을 최소
화하기 위한 전략으로 판단함. 
동사는 이에 더해 2026년 회계연도 기준 약 100조원 규모의 주주환원 계획을 발표했음. 현금배당 30조원 이상, 자사주 
매입 및 소각 60조원 이상으로 구성되며, 기존에 약속했던 잉여현금흐름(FCF)의 50% 이상을 주주에게 환원한다는 원칙
을 구체화한 것임. 지난 8월 발표한 40조원 규모의 자사주 매입은 10월 21일 완료됐으며, 해당 주식은 향후 소각될 예정
임. 이번 3분기 이사회에서는 현금배당 14조원과 추가 자사주 매입 20조원을 결의했으며, 자사주 매입액 중 10조원은 전
량 소각하고 나머지 10조원은 임직원 보상에 활용할 계획임. 임직원 보상용 자사주는 주주환원 규모에서 제외됨. 특히 
향후에도 자사주 매입을 지속할 계획이라는 점에서 주주환원 정책의 지속성이 강화됐다는 판단임.

### Page 3

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

3 
3분기 주당 배당금은 2만원으로, 실적 개선을 반영한 일반배당 1만원과 키옥시아 투자수익을 활용한 특별배당 1만원으
로 구성됨. 총 배당 규모는 약 14조원이며 회사는 올해 실현한 키옥시아 투자수익의 대부분을 3분기 및 연말 특별배당을 
통해 주주에게 환원할 계획임. 이번 결정은 메모리 가격 상승과 고부가 제품 확대에 따른 현금창출력 개선이 실질적인 
주주환원 확대로 연결되고 있음을 보여줌. 특히 2027년 설비투자 확대가 예상되는 상황에서도 대규모 현금배당과 자사
주 소각을 병행한다는 점은 재무적 여력에 대한 자신감을 반영하는 것으로 판단됨. 향후에도 안정적인 실적 성장과 FCF 
확대가 이어질 경우 주주환원의 지속 가능성이 높아질 전망이며 이는 과거 대비 높아진 이익 창출력과 함께 중장기 밸류
에이션 재평가를 뒷받침하는 요인으로 작용할 것으로 예상됨. 
결국 이번 실적발표에서 확인된 핵심은 예상보다 강한 메모리 가격 상승과 HBM4의 본격적인 출하 확대, 그리고 장기계
약을 통한 이익 가시성 개선임. 특히 HBM4의 높은 가격과 안정적인 양산 수율, 기존 DRAM의 가격 상승이 동시에 확
인되면서 2027년 이익 추정치의 추가 상향 가능성이 높아졌다고 판단함. 여기에 LTA 확대에 따른 중장기 이익 안정성 
개선과 대규모 주주환원까지 더해지고 있음. 실적 추정치 상향과 이익의 지속성 개선이 동시에 진행되고 있다는 점에서 
긍정적인 투자 시각을 유지함.

전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 5

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

5 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 09 월 30 일) 
투자등급 매수 중립 매도 합계 
금융투자상품의 비중 96.4% 3.6% 0.0% 100.0%

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
26년 3분기, 물량 감소에도 확인된 이익 방어력 
현대차의 3Q26 매출액은 45조 1,100억원(YoY -3.4%), 영업이익은 2조 6,770억원
(YoY +5.5%), OPM 5.9%(YoY +0.5%p)를 기록했다. 국내 파업 및 영업일수 감소 영
향으로 글로벌 도매 판매량은 92.1만대(YoY -11.3%)까지 감소했으나, HEV 중심의 
고부가 차종 Mix 개선, 기말환율 하락에 따른 판매보증비(YoY -1.2조원) 감소 효과
로 수익성은 오히려 개선됐다. 특히 HEV 판매 비중은 18.2%(YoY +2.7%p)까지 상
승했다. 반면 EV 판매는 전년 대비 감소세가 이어지며 지역별 수요 둔화와 경쟁 심
화 영향이 지속됐다. 
연간 눈높이는 낮아졌지만, 믹스 개선은 지속 
현대차는 글로벌 수요 둔화와 생산 차질을 반영해 2026년 글로벌 도매 판매 목표를 
기존 415.8만대에서 400만대로, 영업이익률 가이던스를 기존 6.3~7.3%에서 
5.5~6.5%로 하향 조정했다. 다만 북미를 중심으로 HEV 판매가 견조하게 유지되고 
있으며, 하반기 주요 신차 투입과 전동화 라인업 확대를 통해 믹스 개선 기조는 이
어질 전망이다. 중장기적으로는 로보택시 공급 확대와 Atlas 상용화 등 Physical AI 
사업도 계획대로 진행 중으로, 단기 판매 성장 둔화에도 자동차 본업의 수익성 방어
와 신사업 모멘텀은 유효한 것으로 판단한다. 
투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 460,000원 하향 
글로벌 완성차 업체들의 수익성 부담은 확대되고 있다. 3분기 자동차 부문 영업이익
률은 Mercedes-Benz 2.5%, Stellantis 0.7%, Volvo -5.6%, VW -7.8%를 기록했다. 
유럽 내 BEV 판매 확대에도 중국 전기차 업체들의 공격적인 해외 진출과 가격 경
쟁, 중국 시장 부진이 동시에 나타나면서 전통 OEM의 수익성 압박이 심화되고 있
다. 이러한 환경에서 현대차는 HEV 중심의 유연한 파워트레인 전략과 북미 중심의 
Mix 개선을 통해 5% 후반의 수익성을 유지하고 있다는 점이 상대적으로 부각된다.

동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.43 / 23 November 2026FOCUS — FRENCH ELECTIONS
A PRIMER ON FISCAL MEASURES DEBATED IN FRENCH ELECTIONS: MOST CANDIDATES COMMIT TO RESTORING FISCAL
SUSTAINABILITY, WITH UNEVEN CREDIBILITY
1 Other growth-critical policy proposals will be reviewed in thematic deep-dives to be published over the coming months.
This note offers an overview of the measures proposed by presidential election candidates from the main political groups,

namely Marine Le Pen, Jean-Luc Mélenchon, Édouard Philippe, Raphaël Glucksmann, David Lisnard and Bruno Retailleau.
The focus here is on their proposals for public finances and pensions 1. Overall, budget plans go in the right direction but
rely on overly optimistic costs and revenue assumptions. However, in several cases a proposed “golden rule” enhances
credibility. The proposals regarding pensions improve on the status quo in most cases, including, in some cases, by intro-

ducing a capitalisation pillar. However, most safeguard costly features of the current system, without enough measures to
offset their impact fully.
KEY TAKEAWAYS
- The bulk of the candidates target a below 3% of GDP fiscal deficit by 2032. The budget proposals are often optimistic (or lack sufficient detail), which is a
common feature of previous presidential campaigns (e.g., in 2017, E. Macron proposed reducing public spending to 50% of GDP by 2022).

- A majority of candidates propose a “golden rule”, including the National Rally (Rassemblement national). A rule would lend credibility to their fiscal path (if
the planned measures are insufficient, the golden rule would require them to adopt additional measures, as it was done e.g. in Germany under the debt brake).
- The budget announcements made by several candidates — including the National Rally (RN) and La France Insoumise (LFI) — are expected to worsen the

fiscal deficit in 2027. According to our calculations, a one-year delay in fiscal consolidation would result in a 2 pp increase in the public debt-to-GDP ratio
by the end of that consolidation (in 2032, debt would reach 126.5% of GDP instead of 124.5% without this delay), and even more if the deficit or interest rate
conditions were to deteriorate significantly.

### Page 2

8
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.43 / 23 November 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
- In the absence of a golden rule, LFI’s commitment to fiscal consolidation remains uncertain. The candidate’s promises are very costly, leading to a clear in-
crease in the fiscal deficit. Some of the proposed measures are confiscatory in nature and would likely shrink the tax base. The candidate proposes no binding
safeguards to ensure that the deficit does not spiral out of control. Furthermore, his proposal to write off part of the public debt held by the ECB could be

interpreted as a way to avoid having to undertake fiscal consolidation efforts to stabilise public debt (not to mention its feasibility).
KEY TAKEAWAYS
- The candidates’ positions on pensions reflect real, underlying progress, particularly in order to mitigate the impact of the repeal of the Borne reform (retire-
ment age back to 62 years old) through savings on other parameters (contribution period, or tightened conditions such as an increased discount per missing

quarter).
- The prospect of fully indexing pensions, as proposed in several candidates' platforms, would be very costly (nearly 2% of GDP).
- Both measures (lowering the retirement age and full indexation) undermine the system’s long-term financial stability. Everything else equal, they also
reduce the size of the active labour force, a headwind to growth and fiscal sustainability.
- Édouard Philippe, David Lisnard and Bruno Retailleau advocate raising the retirement age to 65, and Édouard Philippe also calls for extending the contribu-

tion period to 45 years (while maintaining more favourable treatment for long careers and physically demanding jobs). The savings associated with extending
working careers represent a significant contribution to the announced fiscal consolidation effort.
- With regard to capitalisation, there are marked differences between the candidates who currently rule it out (J.L. Mélenchon, R. Glucksmann) and the others,
who have all made proposals along these lines (including the RN).

Completed on 23 November 2026

AI 产业已深度嵌⼊美国经济，甚⾄到了 “ ⼤⽽不能倒 ” 的地步。 AI 抛售引发衰退恐慌，促使特
朗普政府施压英伟达董事会、⼒挺⻩仁勋复职，推动英伟达股价创纪录暴涨。但这场救市
⾏动正引发国会调查，被指具有 “ ⽔⻔事件 ” 特征，政治⻛险与 AI 估值隐患仍未消退。
摘要：
特朗普政府对英伟达董事会施压、推动⻩仁勋复职⼀事，正从⼀场市场救援⾏动演变为潜在的
政治危机。
据 Fox Business 记者 Charles Gasparino 援引知情⼈⼠消息，促使财政部⻓⻉森特转变⽴场、⼒
挺⻩仁勋复职的核⼼动因，是对经济衰退的恐惧 ——AI 股票的抛售潮令主要投资者向⽩宫周边
⼈⼠传递警告，称美国陷⼊衰退的概率 " 接近 100%" 。⻩仁勋复职消息公布后，英伟达单⽇暴涨
逾 34% ，创 2000 年以来最⼤单⽇涨幅，市值单⽇增加逾 1.1 万亿美元。
然⽽，Gasparino 将特朗普的这⼀操作称为 " 现代版⽔⻔事件 " 。1972 年美国总统⼤选期间，尼
克松竞选团队成员潜⼊⺠主党总部安装窃听器并被捕。此后，⽩宫掩盖真相、阻挠调查的⾏为

曝光，最终迫使尼克松于 1974 年辞职，成为美国历史上⾸位辞职的总统。
Gasparino 警告称国会调查已在路上，⺠主党⼈正将 " ⻩仁勋事件 " 纳⼊弹劾特朗普的议程，短期
市场提振背后潜伏着巨⼤的政治与市场⻛险。
救市逻辑：衰退警告促使⽩宫转向
据 Gasparino 报道，在上周 AI 股票遭到重挫后，主要投资者向⽩宫周边⼈⼠传递警告，认为 AI 交
易的平仓将冲击 " 整个科技⽣态系统 " 的⽀出，经济衰退概率 " 接近 100%" 。这⼀警告被视为⻉森
特急转弯的 " 主要原因 "—— 就在⼋天前，⻉森特还曾要求英伟达董事会在公司外部寻找⻩仁勋的
继任者，⽽到了周⽇，他已将所谓的间谍⻛险斥为 " 某种程度上的⽆中⽣有 " 。
Nomura 分析师 Charlie McElligott 在周⼀给客⼾的报告中指出，AI 与股市已 " ⼤到不能倒 "。他描
述了复职消息触发的机械式市场反应： " 对冲头⼨被强制平仓，空头遭到⽌损，标普期货被拉
爆，市场疯狂追涨。 "
穆迪⾸席经济学家 Mark Zandi 从宏观视⻆提供了另⼀维度的注脚。他估计，今年美国实际 GDP

增⻓中，最多有三分之⼀可归因于 AI 产业繁荣，并表⽰：
Coherence Credit Strategies 的 Sal Naro 向彭博表⽰，这代表了华尔街的共识判断：
市场反弹：机械买盘有限，真实买家仍待⼊场
英伟达单⽇涨幅超 34% 为市场提供了强⼼剂，但分析⼈⼠警告，技术性反弹⼒度有其天花板。
"AI 正在驱动整个经济列⻋。如果 AI 脱轨，整个经济也将随之受到冲击。 "
" 全球 AI 繁荣已经⼤到不容失败。 "

据 Nomura 模型测算，杠杆 ETF 与趋势跟踪基⾦合计形成约 600 亿美元的被动强制买盘。但⼀旦
标普 500 指数突破 7043 点，商品交易顾问（ CTA ）持仓将回归平仓状态，在指数触及 7625 点之
前不再有新增买盘压⼒。此后，市场能否持续上涨，将完全依赖真实资⾦的主动介⼊。
Goldman Sachs 分析师 Anton Tran 在其流动性报告中措辞克制： " 我还不确定市场的命运⼏乎等
同于⼀个⼈的命运，究竟是好事还是坏事。不过，我们并不挑剔。 "
Evercore ISI 的 TMT 交易团队态度则更为明确，其周⼀报告认为：
政治⻛险：国会调查逼近， " ⽔⻔ " 阴影挥之不去
Gasparino 的担忧并不⽌于市场层⾯。他将特朗普此番操作定性为具有 " ⽔⻔事件 " 特征 —— 通过
向董事会施压、在政治问题上 " ⼤做⽂章 " ，为⻩仁勋复职铺路，⽽这⼀路径本⾝已引发反弹。
⻩仁勋将于 12 ⽉ 1 ⽇接受三个众议院委员会的闭⻔录⾳听证，英伟达总法律顾问 Tim Teter 随后于

12 ⽉ 2 ⽇出席。⺠主党⼈要求调阅⻩仁勋与特朗普之间通话的所有记录，⽽特朗普本⼈在公开场
合炫耀⾃⼰已说服董事会，实际上已将这些通话置于调查的核⼼。
Semafor 主编 Ben Smith 认同 Gasparino 对华盛顿政治⾛向的判断；保守派评论⼈ Erick Erickson
亦表⽰，此事 " 将成为⼀个⼤问题，尤其是如果⻩仁勋或 Lutnick 的⼦⼥存在商业利益关联 " 。
英伟达董事会本⾝也未将此事视为已了结：董事会已成⽴委员会，正在聘任⾸席合规官，并表
⽰对该事件的内部调查将持续⾄结案。
⻓期隐患：⻩仁勋回归能否真正⽌住跌势？
⽆论政治如何演变，市场观察⼈⼠对⻩仁勋复职能否从根本上扭转 AI 估值修正存有保留。
Gasparino 本⼈对这⼀救市逻辑持审慎态度，他称此举是 " ⼀次⼤胆的策略，或许在强⼤合⼒的
作⽤下奏效 " ，但同时警告 " ⻛险正在快速积累 " ：
市场策略师 Gary Kaltbaum 对衰退警告的回应更加直⽩：
Gasparino 还转述了市场流传的两种解读：⼀是特朗普此举为记者奉上了 " ⽔⻔事件以来最好的

新闻素材 " ；⼆是⻩仁勋复职不过是在 "AI 交易⻓期已废 " 的背景下，为持仓股东提供了⼀个退出
流动性窗⼝。对此， Gasparino 表⽰： " 我认为两者皆有。 "
以美国家庭持有的股票资产接近 GDP 的 200% 这⼀背景衡量，市场能否在技术性反弹耗尽之后获
得真实资⾦的接⼒，或许才是这场政治与市场博弈最终成败的真正试⾦⽯。
" 当 AI 基础设施建设的历史被书写时，今天理应拥有独⽴的⼀章。这实际上是 AI 产业的第⼀次救
助⾏动 —— 救助的不是某家公司的资产负债表，⽽是⽀撑着⼀场万亿美元级产业建设的底层架
构。"
" ⻩仁勋⽆法再像过去那样做⽣意。国会调查已在路上。 "
" ⽆论如何，该收缩的还是会收缩。 "

### Page 2

2 of 8 October 19, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
The U.S. Capex Boom No One is Talking About—And Its Implications
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
Ariana Chiu, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
We have long been of the belief that the U.S. is on the cusp of an inbound FDI capex boom
that can result in numerous positive spillover effects for the U.S. economy. The latest

figures from the U.S. Department of Commerce bear this out.
Exhibit 1 highlights the growing push among foreign multinationals to be inside the U.S.,
with FDI totaling nearly $240B in the first six months of this year. That is a staggering
103% jump from a year ago and places FDI inflows on track for a record year.
The latest inbound capex already adds to the massive scale of foreign investment in the
U.S. To wit, the accumulated stock of FDI in the U.S. stands at nearly $6T, based on the

latest figures from the Bureau of Economic Analysis (BEA). Manufacturing accounts for
roughly 43% of the total, and, at last count, some 10,000 foreign affiliates were operating
in the U.S.
These firms are largely invisible to the average U.S. investor, but the activities of U.S.
affiliates of foreign multinationals are substantial and competitive- and growth-enhancing.
To this point and based on the latest data from the BEA, foreign affiliates in the U.S.

employed nearly 8.6 million U.S. workers in 2024. That represents 6.1% of total private
sector employment, with manufacturing and retail the two most prominent sectors
of employment. Roughly 24% of all manufacturing jobs in the U.S. are from foreign
subsidiaries. In terms of pay, U.S. workers at these firms make $89,000 annually, 7% higher
than the national average, according to the Global Business Alliance.
Foreign affiliates also contributed some $1.5T to U.S. GDP; comprised some 15.7% of total

U.S. private sector investment; accounted for 12.4% of total U.S. research & development
(R&D) expenditures; paid roughly 20% of all U.S. federal taxes; and made up 22.5% of
U.S. exports and 26.2% of U.S. imports.1 These are the direct effects of U.S. subsidiaries
of foreign companies operating in the U.S. The indirect effects are even greater in terms
of more income, jobs, growth and tax revenue—all bolstering the long-term competitive
advantage of the U.S. In a nutshell, foreign firms operating in the U.S. are an important

ingredient of U.S. economic growth.
There are multiple factors behind surging inflows: One, due to rising U.S. trade
protectionism, foreign multinationals increasingly want to be “inside” the U.S. economy,
not outside. Companies that once served the U.S. market exclusively through exports
increasingly face higher costs and more uncertainties and hence want to be “inside.”
Two, with a $32T-plus economy with extraordinary consumer purchasing power, foreign

firms prefer to be closer to their customers via investment than just trade (exports). There
is no more dynamic consumer in the world than the American shopper.
Three, America’s massive investment cycle in AI, data centers, semiconductors, power
generation, etc., has created numerous investment opportunities for many foreign firms
that don’t want to miss out on America’s surging capex cycle.
Four, and finally, abundant energy supplies, deep capital markets and a highly developed

technological ecosystem have also induced more FDI inflows.
As more U.S. affiliates of foreign multinationals set up shop in the U.S., think more job
creation across the country, more manufacturing output, more locally sourced purchases,
more U.S. exports, more spending on R&D, and more tax revenue for local communities.
And over the long run, think a more globally competitive U.S. economy.
1 Activities of U.S. Affiliates of Foreign Multinational Enterprises, 2024” and “Activities of U.S. Affiliates of Foreign

Multinational Enterprises, 2023”, BEA July 2026.
Investment Implications
The boom in foreign investment in
the U.S. should continue to support
employment, productivity, corporate
earnings, and broader U.S. economic
competitiveness, in our view. Hence
our U.S. bias.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 19, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
And speaking of global competitiveness, Exhibit 2A is very illustrative of the magnet pull of
the U.S. economy to attract FDI versus the rest of the world. Of new greenfield investment
this decade, note the overriding preference for the U.S., accounting for nearly 16% of total
global greenfield investment this decade. America’s share is larger than the next three
countries combined—the United Kingdom, India, and Germany. And note that America’s

share of new investment is more than 5X that of China’s. Most of this new investment
has gone into manufacturing sectors like electrical equipment, computers, appliances and
batteries and has originated from Asia and Europe.
The bulk of foreign investment comes from Europe, followed by Japan and Canada, but as
Exhibit 2B highlights, there is a new dynamic playing out when it comes to America’s ability
to attract capital. There is a new source in town: a handful of developing nations (namely

Taiwan, South Korea, Mexico and Brazil). Mexican manufacturers, Taiwan technology
leaders, South Korea chip leaders, Brazil industrial firms, India pharmaceutical firms—these
companies and more are realizing that in today’s trade environment, the best way to serve
the American market is not necessarily through exports but through investment—investing
locally. FDI flows from developing nations reached $63B in the 12 months through June,
more than double the level a year prior ($24.5B), according to the BEA. Fueled by insatiable

AI demand, flows from South Korea alone totaled $23.4B in the first half of 2026. The
upshot is that an increasingly important source of capital for the U.S. is coming from an
unlikely source: the developing nations.
More fire for the capex boom. The coming boom in FDI inflows is supportive of our thesis
that the U.S. stands on the cusp of a major industrial upgrade. We are in the early innings
of a structural—not cyclical—capex cycle that should lead to the rebuilding of domestic

supply chains, higher value-added manufacturing, and more AI-driven productivity growth.
Foreign firms, in our opinion, will be part of this process.
That is important to recognize because remember the following: when a foreign company
builds a factory or an R&D facility or expands its existing manufacturing capacity right
here in the U.S., good things happen. Demand for U.S. goods and services goes up (as does
demand for construction, materials, logistics and professional services). New jobs are created,

boosting incomes of U.S. workers, while supporting more local spending and consumption.
And because many foreign firms often bring advanced manufacturing technologies when
investing in the U.S., the end results include a boost in productivity and innovation in various
sectors of the economy.
All the above, of course, are the secret ingredients to stronger U.S. economic growth, rising
corporate earnings and higher equity prices. With a little help from our friends, America’s

industrial renewal is gaining strength and momentum.

MARKET VIEW
Europe’s Electoral Cycle and Market Risks
Ehiwario Efeyini, Director and Senior Investment Strategist
As midterm elections approach for the U.S. next month, voters across Europe will also
enter their own election season starting this November and continuing throughout
2027. Over the next year, 52% of the total EU population, representing 46% of EU GDP
across eight countries, will go to the polls, including voters in three of its four largest

economies (Exhibit 3). Having announced snap general elections earlier this month,
Spain will vote for members of its lower and upper house on November 29. A series of
parliamentary elections are then due for 2027 in Estonia (by March 7), Finland (on April
18), Greece (by July 25), Slovakia (by September 28), Poland (by November 11) and Italy
(by December 22). And perhaps most consequentially France will stage Presidential
elections on April 18, with a potential second round of voting set for May 2.

For the EU as a whole, the significance of these coming elections will be the potential
for a shift toward more populist, domestically oriented policy priorities from leading
opposition candidates (particularly in France, Spain and Poland) which could obstruct the
process of closer EU integration. In the nearer term, adoption of the new seven-year
(2028-2034) EU budget due by the end of 2027 could be impeded. And over the longer
term, renewed efforts toward an EU-wide capital market union to deepen and attract

more inward investment into European markets could also be further delayed.
At the country level, investors have seen bond yields rise globally in 2026 on the back of
higher inflation and concerns over fiscal sustainability. The debt question has arguably
been most pressing in parts of Europe, especially given the likelihood that upcoming
elections make for domestic increases in government largesse. And of the eight
countries going to the polls over the next year, France remains the biggest source of

concern. Interest rate spreads on French 10-year sovereign bonds have overtaken those
in both Greece and Italy this year (Exhibit 4), driven by investor concerns over public
debt. The 2027 government budget unveiled earlier this month projected a deficit of
5.0% of GDP (well in excess of the 3% specified in the EU Stability and Growth Pact),
but remained insufficiently expansive to nullify the risk that the current government
could be unseated later this year if it is forced through the fragmented parliament. This

comes on top of an existing debt load of close to 120% of GDP and a halving of official
2026 growth forecasts from 1.0% at the start of the year to 0.5% in September. In
short, all four variables in France’s debt sustainability equation currently stand as major
headwinds, and the two leading candidates in the 2027 election have shown limited
appetite for fiscal consolidation over the coming years.
Exhibit 3: Europe's Upcoming Elections Represent Around Half of EU GDP and Population.

Investment Implications
We remain cautious on European
markets overall, despite seeing
pockets of strength in selected
areas such as defense, data center
infrastructure and renewable energy.
On top of growing uncertainty
from the upcoming electoral cycle

and interest rate outlook, rising
competition and slowing demand
from China continue to challenge
key segments of the Consumer
Discretionary sector. And limited
direct AI concentration also leaves
Europe under-exposed to the key
growth theme that continues to buoy
equity markets in the U.S., Japan and
emerging Asia.

Intervention from the ECB under its 2022 bond purchase program is also unlikely at this

stage given that the spread widening in France has not appeared either disorderly or
unwarranted. Meanwhile, conditions could potentially be aggravated by expected ECB rate
hikes in response to still above-target inflation across the eurozone. Indeed recent moves
to tighten monetary policy from a number of major central banks have been read most
hawkishly in Europe. Most of the increase in European bond yields has come from higher
inflation breakevens as opposed to real yields in the U.S. and Japan. And despite some

paring back of tightening expectations in the wake of recent bond volatility, the market is
still pricing three more policy rate increases over the next 12 months.
Euro depreciation and an emerging shift toward more protectionist trade policy with China
could further complicate the ECB policy outlook. China posted a record $35B trade surplus,
according to Eurostat, with the EU in September (alongside its $938B total surplus for the
first three quarters of 2026), and the two markets have agreed to reduce the EU bilateral

deficit by capping imports of hybrid cars for four years. Hybrids account for less than 2%
of total EU imports from China, but this could potentially mark the start of a broader shift
toward more trade protection in Europe with its largest foreign supplier which risks adding
further upward pressure to cost-push inflation. And though the leading candidates in the
French election may favor resuming oil and gas imports from Russia, Europe also remains

among the most at risk from the global energy shock. Indeed some 56% of the region’s
crude oil consumption is in the form of diesel product according to the International Energy
Agency, more than twice the U.S. share.
The 2- to 10-year eurozone yield curve has already flattened significantly since the start
of the year. And at just over 30 basis points as of mid-October, it remains flatter than
its equivalents in the U.S., UK and Japan. Outside Energy and Technology, Financials have

been among the strongest-performing sectors in Europe this year. But additional ECB
rate hikes could lead to further curve flattening, compressing bank net interest margins
and contributing to slower growth—particularly given that rate increases are coming in
response to supply-driven inflation rather than reflecting stronger economic activity.
We nonetheless continue to see pockets of strength in European markets. Growing
defense budgets should remain a tailwind for Industrials as national governments look to

meet their North Atlantic Treaty Organization targets over the next several years. And the
sector should be further supported by indirect exposure to AI investment through electric
cabling, power infrastructure and building materials for data centers. Leaders in renewable
energy equipment and services are also likely to be beneficiaries of grid upgrades and
broadening electrification globally, particularly given elevated fossil fuel prices. But our view

on the region remains cautious overall. On top of growing uncertainty from the upcoming
electoral cycle and interest rate outlook, rising competition and slowing demand from
China continue to challenge key segments of Europe’s Consumer Discretionary sector.
Limited direct AI concentration in technology hardware, cloud services and application
software also leaves Europe underexposed to the key growth theme that continues to buoy
equity markets in the U.S., Japan and emerging Asia.

THOUGHT OF THE WEEK
Cautious Optimism Entering Fiscal Year 2027
Ariana Chiu, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
“A renewed focus on rebuilding fiscal space is urgent, given elevated debt, higher
borrowing costs, multiplying demands on public budgets and heightened uncertainty.” So
warned the IMF about the global fiscal outlook last week in its updated World Economic

Outlook report. Much is at stake for developed nations in Europe and Asia facing high
existing debt, unfavorable demographics and more modest growth.
For the U.S., between structurally higher mandatory outlays, the administration’s $1.5T
proposed defense package, and the 10-year U.S. Treasury yield sitting above 5%, spending
is likely headed higher. We continue to fade concerns of an impending U.S. debt crisis,
however, largely because economic growth continues to surprise to the upside. Expanding

output is good news for the debt-to-GDP ratio, which remains a touch below 100%.
Indeed, rather than being led higher on account of un-anchored inflation expectations, the
10-year U.S. Treasury yield has repriced to reflect better-than-expected nominal growth
(Exhibit 5A). Higher yields in the face of slowing growth, meanwhile, would be a different
story, not only for the fiscal outlook (worse) but also for U.S. equity valuations (lower).
Of course, after the U.S. accumulated another $2T deficit in FY 2026,2 “rebuilding fiscal

space” in the IMF’s words will be politically unpopular and mathematically challenging.
In our view, the biggest near-term tail risk would be a surprise move lower in growth as
budget deficits at 6% of GDP in good times leave little room for bad times. This isn’t our
base case given robust consumer spending per our internal credit and debit card data and
ongoing investment in the AI buildout and beyond. We expect these two pillars to sustain
growth in 2027.

The other good news is that, contrary to concerns, demand for Treasurys remains robust.
Yields are attractive—that was the message from October’s successful $39B 10-year
Treasury note auction. At the same time, U.S. debt boasts an array of buyers: domestic
households, the Federal Reserve, mutual funds and exchange-traded funds, money market
funds, pension funds, foreign central banks, private investors overseas, and more. Per
Exhibit 5B, whereas foreign official investors once dominated some 40% of the U.S.

Treasury market during the 2008/2009 Great Financial Crisis, their share has fallen to
12% while domestic private investors now account for a majority of holdings. Translated:
demand is more diverse than most appreciate.
Exhibit 5: Higher Yields Meet Shifting Composition of U.S. Treasury Demand.
A) Higher 10-year Yield Consistent with Strong Nominal Growth Environment. B) Domestic Private Investors Now Dominate U.S. Treasury Market.

Investment Implications
In our view, higher nominal and real
yields present a compelling entry point
for investors seeking income and can
provide cushion in the face of higher
rate volatility. We expect demand for
U.S. assets to remain healthy and favor
the U.S. over developed economies in
Europe facing more modest growth and
fiscal instability.

Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

Document 344: France’s golden rule won’t fix its budget problem

Source PDF: all report pdf/France’s golden rule won’t fix its budget problem.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-24
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

French President Emmanuel Macron’s proposal to revive a
constitutional ﬁscal rule has helped ease pressure on French
government bonds. But the reform would only create a
framework whose strength would depend on legislation
adopted later. The immediate test of France’s ﬁscal credibility
remains the 2027 budget
Charlotte de Montpellier
Macron’s golden rule may reassure markets, but it cannot replace a credible
ﬁscal plan. The immediate test will be France’s 2027 budget

What exactly would change?
Macron’s surprise proposal came at the start of another high-risk
week for France, after the European Commission forecast last week
that the deﬁcit would reach 5.4% of GDP in 2027 and 5.7% in 2028,
and ahead of S&P’s rating assessment on Friday. It would revive a
constitutional amendment approved by the National Assembly
and the Senate in 2011 – during the eurozone sovereign debt crisis
– but never submitted to Congress for ﬁnal approval.
